# Skills · C · part 193

874 entries, compound-spectra-data-model-mapping to computational-anomaly-detection-testing. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **compound-spectra-data-model-mapping**: Use when a laboratory needs to ingest LC-MS/MS (or NMR) spectra with their metadata into a local PeakForest database via the prefilled spreadsheet import template, associating each spectrum with a compound and a biological matrix such as human urine. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/compound-spectra-data-model-mapping))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/compound-spectra-deisotoping-annotation))

It renders the evidence with every claim carrying its label - FACT, DERIVED, MANAGEMENT_CLAIM, ESTIMATE, or UNVERIFIED - traceable back to an original source, keeps data gaps and unresolved questions visible instead of smoothing them over, and never upgrades a management claim into a fact while writing. - **computation**: 当用户要判断一件事「能不能用计算机做、要花多大代价、边界在哪里」时调用。 覆盖《计算》(吴翰清) 的方法面：计算概念定层与混淆诊断、可计算性→代价→NP 完全三级筛选、计算系统的先天约束清单、编码—规则—初始状态三步建模、自指系统的编码+对角线+归谬诊断、形式系统四性质体检、有穷化改写、哥德尔式编码、图灵完备判定、问题复杂度类定位七步、归约论证、近似算法与启发式设计、并行化加速比、量子计算宣传可信度、复杂系统诊断、组织与智能系统的计算化翻译、问题定义与排障、争议问题资产化、计算主义与机器智能的信念边界。 不适用于：具体编程语言与框架的教学、算法实现与性能调优细节、哲学立场裁断、需要临床/法律/金融等专业资质的处置。 ([AholicK/zh-book-skills](https://github.com/AholicK/zh-book-skills/tree/HEAD/computation))
- **computation-analysis**: Analyze computation-intensive operators and performance for Ascend NPU. Use when examining model operations, performance bottlenecks, and CANN operator library support. ([FeRhodium/ascend-migration](https://github.com/FeRhodium/ascend-migration/tree/HEAD/skills/computation-analysis) · ★7)
- **computation-audit**: Design, run, or audit a mathematical computation supporting a research claim when correctness, provenance, tested range, reproducibility, or interpretation matters. Includes symbolic, exact, finite-field, representation-theoretic, homological and numerical experiments. Do not use to verify a cited theorem, audit a proof with no load-bearing computation, pursue a whole research route, or for general programming. ([nidrissi/mathbox](https://github.com/nidrissi/mathbox/tree/HEAD/skills/computation-audit) · ★17)

- **computation-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when comparing computational cost of an MSI analysis between raw and peak-picked spectra, or when documenting runtime of a Cardinal computation as evidence that pre-processing yields a speedup. - **computation-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when comparing wall-clock runtimes of alternative NetGSA computational variants (NetGSA.REHE.Cl, NetGSA.REHE, NetGSA.REML.All) on cancer gene expression datasets, or when validating that an overhauled pipeline meets a runtime budget (minutes on a personal computer). - **computation-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when a pipeline's computational time (a stated limitation of RCC) can be traded against clustering accuracy by comparing parallel low-repeat runs against a single high-repeat run with ARI and timing measurements. - **computation-time-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantify the computational efficiency of an ensemble deep learning method (e.g. SnapCCESS) versus a conventional ensemble baseline by timing training phases over different epochs and ensemble sizes.
